{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /etc/claude-code/.claude/skills/manufacturing-failure-reason-codebook-normalization\n\nThis skill should be considered when you need to normalize, standardize, or correct testing engineers' written failure reasons to match the requirements provided in the product codebooks. Common errors in engineer-written reasons include ambiguous descriptions, missing important words, improper personal writing habits, using wrong abbreviations, improper combining multiple reasons into one sentence without clear spacing or in wrong order, writing wrong station names or model, writing typos, improper combining Chinese and English characters, cross-project differences, and taking wrong products' codebook.\n\nSome codes are defined for specific stations and cannot be used by other stations. If entry.stations is not None, the predicted code should only be considered valid when the record station matches one of the stations listed in entry.stations. Otherwise, the code should be rejected. For each record segment, the system evaluates candidate codes defined in the corresponding product codebook and computes an internal matching score for each candidate. You should consider multiple evidence sources to calculate the score to measure how well a candidate code explains the segment, and normalize the score to a stable range [0.0, 1.0]. Evidence can include text evidence from raw_reason_text (e.g., overlap or fuzzy similarity between span_text and codebook text such as standard_label, keywords_examples, or categories), station compatibility, fail_code alignment, test_item alignment, and conflict cues such as mutually exclusive or contradictory signals. After all candidate codes are scored, sort them in descending order. Let c1 be the top candidate with score s1 and c2 be the second candidate with score s2. When multiple candidates fall within a small margin of the best score, the system applies a deterministic tie-break based on record context (e.g., record_id, segment index, station, fail_code, test_item) to avoid always choosing the same code in near-tie cases while keeping outputs reproducible. To provide convincing answers, add station, fail_code, test_item, a short token overlap cue, or a component reference to the rationale.\n\nUNKNOWN handling: UNKNOWN should be decided based on the best match only (i.e., after ranking), not by marking multiple candidates. If the best-match score is low (weak evidence), output pred_code=\"UNKNOWN\" and pred_label=\"\" to give engineering an alert. When strong positive cues exist (e.g., clear component references), UNKNOWN should be less frequent than in generic or noisy segments.\n\nConfidence calibration: confidence ranges from 0.0 to 1.0 and reflects an engineering confidence level (not a probability). Calibrate confidence from match quality so that UNKNOWN predictions are generally less confident than non-UNKNOWN predictions, and confidence values are not nearly constant. Confidence should show distribution-level separation between UNKNOWN and non-UNKNOWN predictions (e.g., means, quantiles, and diversity), and should be weakly aligned with evidence strength; round confidence to 4 decimals.\n\nHere is a pipeline reference\n1) Load test_center_logs.csv into logs_rows and load each product codebook; build valid_code_set, station_scope_map, and CodebookEntry objects.  \n2) For each record, split raw_reason_text into 1–N segments; each segment uses segment_id=<record_id>-S<i> and keeps an exact substring as span_text.  \n3) For each segment, filter candidates by station scope, then compute match score from combined evidence (text evidence, station compatibility, context alignment, and conflict cues).  \n4) Rank candidates by score; if multiple are within a small margin of the best, choose deterministically using a context-dependent tie-break among near-best station-compatible candidates.  \n5) Output exactly one pred_code/pred_label per segment from the product codebook (or UNKNOWN/\"\" when best evidence is weak) and compute confidence by calibrating match quality with sufficient diversity; round to 4 decimals.\n"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"2690ed81-b4aa-4bfc-8106-b9e7d742d859","uuid":"c7e66101-1058-47e7-8b5b-9cc8459af20b","isSynthetic":true}

record_id: str) -> List[Segment]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Segment raw_reason_text into multiple segments based on delimiters.\n    Common delimiters: /, ;, &, +, 、, ，, ;, and logical separators.\n    \"\"\"\n    if not raw_text or raw_text.strip() == '':\n        return []\n\n    # Patterns that indicate segment boundaries\n    # Order matters - longer patterns first\n    segment_patterns = [\n        r'\\s*/\\s*',           # Forward slash with optional spaces\n        r'\\s*;\\s*',           # Semicolon\n        r'\\s*；\\s*',          # Chinese semicolon\n        r'\\s*&\\s*',           # Ampersand\n        r'\\s*\\+\\s*',          # Plus sign\n        r'\\s*、\\s*',          # Chinese enumeration comma\n        r'\\s*，\\s*(?=[^\\d])', # Chinese comma (not before numbers)\n    ]\n\n    # Combine all patterns\n    combined_pattern = '|'.join(segment_patterns)\n\n    # Split the text\n    parts = re.split(combined_pattern, raw_text)\n\n    # Filter out empty segments and noise words\n    noise_words = {'NG', 'OK了', '已处理', '浮高', '挂', '有问题', '可能', '疑似',\n                   '接触不好', '不稳定', '确认', '返修', '换掉', '复测', '重测过',\n                   'rework', 'retest', 'suspect', 'intermittent', '建议', '按流程'}\n\n    segments = []\n    current_pos = 0\n\n    for i, part in enumerate(parts):\n        part = part.strip()\n        if not part:\n            continue\n\n        # Find the actual position in the original text\n        start_idx = raw_text.find(part, current_pos)\n        if start_idx == -1:\n            # Try to find partial match\n            start_idx = current_pos\n        end_idx = start_idx + len(part)\n        current_pos = end_idx\n\n        # Skip if it's just a noise word\n        if part in noise_words or len(part) < 3:\n            continue\n\n        # Check if this segment contains meaningful content\n        # Remove trailing noise words\n        clean_part = part\n        for noise in noise_words:\n            if clean_part.endswith(' ' + noise) or clean_part.endswith(noise):\n                clean_part = clean_part.rsplit(noise, 1)[0].strip()\n\n        if len(clean_part) < 3:\n            continue\n\n        segment_id = f\"{record_id}-S{len(segments) + 1}\"\n        segments.append(Segment(\n            segment_id=segment_id,\n            span_text=part,  # Keep original text with noise for traceability\n            start_idx=start_idx,\n            end_idx=end_idx\n        ))\n\n    # If no segments found, treat the whole text as one segment\n    if not segments and len(raw_text.strip()) >= 3:\n        segments.append(Segment(\n            segment_id=f\"{record_id}-S1\",\n            span_text=raw_text.strip(),\n            start_idx=0,\n            end_idx=len(raw_text)\n        ))\n\n    return segments\n\n\ndef fuzzy_match_score(text1: str, text2: str) -> float:\n    \"\"\"Calculate fuzzy string matching score between two texts.\"\"\"\n    # Normalize texts\n    t1 = text1.lower().strip()\n    t2 = text2.lower().strip()\n\n    # Direct match\n    if t1 == t2:\n        return 1.0\n\n    # Use SequenceMatcher for similarity\n    ratio = SequenceMatcher(None, t1, t2).ratio()\n    return ratio\n\n\ndef keyword_match_score(text: str, keywords: List[str]) -> Tuple[float, List[str]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate how many keywords match the text.\n    Returns score and list of matched keywords.\n    \"\"\"\n    text_lower = text.lower()\n    matched = []\n\n    for kw in keywords:\n        kw_lower = kw.lower().strip()\n        if kw_lower in text_lower:\n            matched.append(kw)\n\n    if not keywords:\n        return 0.0, []\n\n    score = len(matched) / len(keywords)\n    return score, matched\n\n\ndef category_match_patterns() -> Dict[str, List[Tuple[str, float]]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Return patterns that indicate specific failure categories.\n    Each pattern has a base score weight.\n    \"\"\"\n    return {\n        'OPEN': [\n            (r'开路|open|OS.*open|OS，像开路', 0.9),\n            (r'虚焊|cold.?solder|少锡', 0.7),\n        ],\n        'SHORT': [\n            (r'短路|short|桥连|连锡|solder.?bridge', 0.9),\n            (r'OS.*short|OS，像短路', 0.85),\n        ],\n        'COLD_SOLDER': [\n            (r'虚焊|cold.?solder|浮高|intermittent', 0.9),\n            (r'功能不稳', 0.7),\n        ],\n        'FIXTURE_CONTACT': [\n            (r'接触不良|fixture.?contact|重插后偶尔OK|reseat', 0.95),\n            (r'治具|探针|pogo', 0.8),\n        ],\n        'PROBE_WORN': [\n            (r'探针磨损|probe.?worn|换针|replace.?pin', 0.95),\n        ],\n        'FLASH_FAIL': [\n            (r'烧录失败|写入失败|program.*fail|flash.*fail|重烧不稳定', 0.95),\n        ],\n        'CHECKSUM_FAIL': [\n            (r'校验失败|CRC|checksum|verify.?fail', 0.95),\n        ],\n        'BOOT_FAIL': [\n            (r'启动.*失败|boot.*fail|OS.?fail|cannot.?boot|卡logo|进不了系统', 0.95),\n        ],\n        'VERSION_MISMATCH': [\n            (r'版本不符|下错程序|version.?mismatch|wrong.?FW', 0.95),\n        ],\n        'LEAKAGE': [\n            (r'漏电|leakage|HiPot|hipot|绝缘不良', 0.95),\n        ],\n        'CURRENT_HIGH': [\n            (r'静态电流.*高|idle.?current.*high|功耗高|电流偏高', 0.95),\n            (r'短路趋势|器件异常', 0.7),\n        ],\n        'CAL_DATA_FAIL': [\n            (r'校准.*失败|cal.*fail|RF.*校准|重校仍挂', 0.95),\n        ],\n        'CONTAMINATION': [\n            (r'污染|助焊剂|残留|contamination|residue', 0.9),\n        ],\n        'REVERSED': [\n            (r'反向|极性|polarity|reversed|装反', 0.95),\n        ],\n        'PRESSURE_MARK': [\n            (r'压伤|pressure.?mark|pad.?damage', 0.95),\n        ],\n        'ESD': [\n            (r'ESD|静电', 0.95),\n        ],\n        'MISSING': [\n            (r'少件|missing', 0.95),\n        ],\n        'CONNECTOR_WEAR': [\n            (r'connector.?wear|插拔|insertion.?loss', 0.9),\n        ],\n    }\n\n\ndef extract_component_reference(text: str) -> Optional[str]:\n    \"\"\"Extract component reference from text (e.g., R152, U12, C87).\"\"\"\n    # Pattern for component references\n    patterns = [\n        r'\\b([RUCDQLJ]\\d+)\\b',           # R152, U12, C87, D34, Q15, L5, J3\n        r'\\b(TP\\d+)\\b',                   # TP5\n        r'\\b(ANT\\d*)\\b',                  # ANT1\n        r'\\b(SH\\d+)\\b',                   # SH1\n        r'\\b([A-Z]\\d+_[A-Z0-9]+)\\b',      # U12_VDD style\n    ]\n\n    for pattern in patterns:\n        match = re.search(pattern, text, re.IGNORECASE)\n        if match:\n            return match.group(1).upper()\n    return None\n\n\ndef extract_net_reference(text: str) -> Optional[str]:\n    \"\"\"Extract net/signal reference from text.\"\"\"\n    net_patterns = [\n        r'\\b(I2C_SDA|I2C_SCL|SPI_MISO|SPI_MOSI|RESET_N|PGOOD|SW_NODE|VBUS_SNS|VDD_\\w+)\\b',\n        r'\\b(R152_NET|U12_VDD|ANT_FEED|RF_IN|RF_OUT)\\b',\n        r'\\b(JTAG_CHAIN|BOUNDARY_\\w+|SCAN_IDCODE)\\b',\n        r'\\b(FLASH_Program|HIPOT_Leakage|RF_\\w+)\\b',\n        r'\\b(Thermal_Trip|Brownout_Reset|Idle_Current)\\b',\n    ]\n\n    for pattern in net_patterns:\n        match = re.search(pattern, text, re.IGNORECASE)\n        if match:\n            return match.group(1)\n    return None\n\n\ndef score_candidate(\n    segment: Segment,\n    entry: CodebookEntry,\n    record: LogRecord,\n    category_patterns: Dict[str, List[Tuple[str, float]]]\n) -> Tuple[float, str]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Score how well a codebook entry matches a segment.\n    Returns (score, rationale).\n    \"\"\"\n    text = segment.span_text\n    score = 0.0\n    evidence = []\n\n    # 1. Keyword matching (weight: 0.35)\n    kw_score, matched_kws = keyword_match_score(text, entry.keywords)\n    score += kw_score * 0.35\n    if matched_kws:\n        evidence.append(f\"keywords: {', '.join(matched_kws[:3])}\")\n\n    # 2. Category pattern matching (weight: 0.25)\n    category_key = entry.category_lv2\n    if category_key in category_patterns:\n        patterns = category_patterns[category_key]\n        best_pattern_score = 0.0\n        for pattern, weight in patterns:\n            if re.search(pattern, text, re.IGNORECASE):\n                best_pattern_score = max(best_pattern_score, weight)\n        score += best_pattern_score * 0.25\n        if best_pattern_score > 0:\n            evidence.append(f\"category={entry.category_lv2}\")\n\n    # 3. Standard label similarity (weight: 0.20)\n    label_score = fuzzy_match_score(text, entry.standard_label)\n    score += label_score * 0.20\n\n    # 4. Station compatibility (weight: 0.10)\n    if entry.station_scope:\n        if record.station in entry.station_scope:\n            score += 0.10\n            evidence.append(f\"station={record.station}\")\n        else:\n            # Penalty for station mismatch\n            score -= 0.05\n\n    # 5. Component/Net reference alignment (weight: 0.10)\n    segment_component = extract_component_reference(text)\n    segment_net = extract_net_reference(text)\n    label_component = extract_component_reference(entry.standard_label)\n    label_net = extract_net_reference(entry.standard_label)\n\n    if segment_component and label_component:\n        if segment_component == label_component:\n            score += 0.10\n            evidence.append(f\"component={segment_component}\")\n        else:\n            score -= 0.02\n\n    if segment_net and label_net:\n        if segment_net.upper() == label_net.upper():\n            score += 0.08\n\n    # 6. Fail code alignment (bonus)\n    if record.fail_code:\n        # Check if fail code indicates specific failure type\n        fail_code_prefix = record.fail_code[0] if record.fail_code else ''\n        if fail_code_prefix == 'E' and entry.category_lv1 == 'ELECTRICAL':\n            score += 0.03\n        elif fail_code_prefix == 'F' and entry.category_lv1 == 'FW_SW':\n            score += 0.03\n        elif fail_code_prefix == 'H' and 'HIPOT' in entry.standard_label.upper():\n            score += 0.03\n        elif fail_code_prefix == 'B' and 'BURNIN' in str(entry.station_scope):\n            score += 0.03\n        elif fail_code_prefix == 'S' and 'BST' in str(entry.station_scope):\n            score += 0.03\n        elif fail_code_prefix == 'R' and ('RF' in entry.standard_label or 'CAL' in entry.category_lv2):\n            score += 0.03\n\n        if record.fail_code:\n            evidence.append(f\"fail_code={record.fail_code}\")\n\n    # 7. Test item alignment\n    if record.test_item:\n        if record.test_item in entry.standard_label or record.test_item in text:\n            score += 0.02\n            evidence.append(f\"test_item={record.test_item}\")\n\n    # Normalize score to [0, 1]\n    score = max(0.0, min(1.0, score))\n\n    rationale = \"; \".join(evidence) if evidence else \"generic match\"\n\n    return score, rationale\n\n\ndef deterministic_tiebreak(\n    candidates: List[Tuple[float, CodebookEntry, str]],\n    record: LogRecord,\n    segment: Segment,\n    margin: float = 0.05\n) -> Tuple[CodebookEntry, str]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Deterministic tie-breaking when multiple candidates are within margin of best score.\n    Uses record context to make reproducible choices.\n    \"\"\"\n    if not candidates:\n        return None, \"\"\n\n    best_score = candidates[0][0]\n    near_best = [(score, entry, rationale) for score, entry, rationale in candidates\n                 if best_score - score <= margin]\n\n    if len(near_best) == 1:\n        return near_best[0][1], near_best[0][2]\n\n    # Create a deterministic hash from context\n    context_str = f\"{record.record_id}:{segment.segment_id}:{record.station}:{record.fail_code}:{record.test_item}\"\n    hash_val = int(hashlib.md5(context_str.encode()).hexdigest(), 16)\n\n    # Select based on hash\n    selected_idx = hash_val % len(near_best)\n    return near_best[selected_idx][1], near_best[selected_idx][2]\n\n\ndef calibrate_confidence(\n    match_score: float,\n    is_unknown: bool,\n    record: LogRecord,\n    segment: Segment\n) -> float:\n    \"\"\"\n    Calibrate confidence from match score.\n    UNKNOWN predictions should generally be less confident than non-UNKNOWN.\n    \"\"\"\n    if is_unknown:\n        # UNKNOWN confidence: lower range [0.15, 0.45]\n        # Scale based on how close to threshold\n        base_conf = 0.15 + (match_score * 0.30)\n        # Add small deterministic variation\n        context_hash = hash(f\"{record.record_id}:{segment.segment_id}\")\n        variation = (context_hash % 100) / 1000  # 0.000 to 0.099\n        confidence = base_conf + (variation * 0.15)\n        confidence = min(0.45, max(0.15, confidence))\n    else:\n        # Non-UNKNOWN confidence: higher range [0.50, 0.98]\n        # Scale based on match quality\n        if match_score >= 0.8:\n            base_conf = 0.85 + (match_score - 0.8) * 0.65  # 0.85-0.98\n        elif match_score >= 0.6:\n            base_conf = 0.65 + (match_score - 0.6) * 1.0   # 0.65-0.85\n        elif match_score >= 0.4:\n            base_conf = 0.50 + (match_score - 0.4) * 0.75  # 0.50-0.65\n        else:\n            base_conf = 0.50\n\n        # Add deterministic variation for diversity\n        context_hash = hash(f\"{record.record_id}:{segment.segment_id}\")\n        variation = (context_hash % 100) / 500  # 0.00 to 0.198\n        confidence = base_conf + (variation * 0.10) - 0.05\n        confidence = min(0.98, max(0.50, confidence))\n\n    return round(confidence, 4)\n\n\ndef normalize_segment(\n    segment: Segment,\n    record: LogRecord,\n    codebook: List[CodebookEntry],\n    category_patterns: Dict[str, List[Tuple[str, float]]],\n    unknown_threshold: float = 0.30\n) -> PredictionResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Normalize a single segment to a codebook entry.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Filter candidates by station scope\n    valid_candidates = []\n    for entry in codebook:\n        if entry.station_scope and record.station not in entry.station_scope:\n            continue\n        score, rationale = score_candidate(segment, entry, record, category_patterns)\n        valid_candidates.append((score, entry, rationale))\n\n    # record_id: str) -> List[Segment]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Segment raw_reason_text into multiple segments based on delimiters.\n    Common delimiters: /, ;, &, +, 、, ，, ;, and logical separators.\n    \"\"\"\n    if not raw_text or raw_text.strip() == '':\n        return []\n\n    # Patterns that indicate segment boundaries\n    # Order matters - longer patterns first\n    segment_patterns = [\n        r'\\s*/\\s*',           # Forward slash with optional spaces\n        r'\\s*;\\s*',           # Semicolon\n        r'\\s*；\\s*',          # Chinese semicolon\n        r'\\s*&\\s*',           # Ampersand\n        r'\\s*\\+\\s*',          # Plus sign\n        r'\\s*、\\s*',          # Chinese enumeration comma\n        r'\\s*，\\s*(?=[^\\d])', # Chinese comma (not before numbers)\n    ]\n\n    # Combine all patterns\n    combined_pattern = '|'.join(segment_patterns)\n\n    # Split the text\n    parts = re.split(combined_pattern, raw_text)\n\n    # Filter out empty segments and noise words\n    noise_words = {'NG', 'OK了', '已处理', '浮高', '挂', '有问题', '可能', '疑似',\n                   '接触不好', '不稳定', '确认', '返修', '换掉', '复测', '重测过',\n                   'rework', 'retest', 'suspect', 'intermittent', '建议', '按流程'}\n\n    segments = []\n    current_pos = 0\n\n    for i, part in enumerate(parts):\n        part = part.strip()\n        if not part:\n            continue\n\n        # Find the actual position in the original text\n        start_idx = raw_text.find(part, current_pos)\n        if start_idx == -1:\n            # Try to find partial match\n            start_idx = current_pos\n        end_idx = start_idx + len(part)\n        current_pos = end_idx\n\n        # Skip if it's just a noise word\n        if part in noise_words or len(part) < 3:\n            continue\n\n        # Check if this segment contains meaningful content\n        # Remove trailing noise words\n        clean_part = part\n        for noise in noise_words:\n            if clean_part.endswith(' ' + noise) or clean_part.endswith(noise):\n                clean_part = clean_part.rsplit(noise, 1)[0].strip()\n\n        if len(clean_part) < 3:\n            continue\n\n        segment_id = f\"{record_id}-S{len(segments) + 1}\"\n        segments.append(Segment(\n            segment_id=segment_id,\n            span_text=part,  # Keep original text with noise for traceability\n            start_idx=start_idx,\n            end_idx=end_idx\n        ))\n\n    # If no segments found, treat the whole text as one segment\n    if not segments and len(raw_text.strip()) >= 3:\n        segments.append(Segment(\n            segment_id=f\"{record_id}-S1\",\n            span_text=raw_text.strip(),\n            start_idx=0,\n            end_idx=len(raw_text)\n        ))\n\n    return segments\n\n\ndef fuzzy_match_score(text1: str, text2: str) -> float:\n    \"\"\"Calculate fuzzy string matching score between two texts.\"\"\"\n    # Normalize texts\n    t1 = text1.lower().strip()\n    t2 = text2.lower().strip()\n\n    # Direct match\n    if t1 == t2:\n        return 1.0\n\n    # Use SequenceMatcher for similarity\n    ratio = SequenceMatcher(None, t1, t2).ratio()\n    return ratio\n\n\ndef keyword_match_score(text: str, keywords: List[str]) -> Tuple[float, List[str]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate how many keywords match the text.\n    Returns score and list of matched keywords.\n    \"\"\"\n    text_lower = text.lower()\n    matched = []\n\n    for kw in keywords:\n        kw_lower = kw.lower().strip()\n        if kw_lower in text_lower:\n            matched.append(kw)\n\n    if not keywords:\n        return 0.0, []\n\n    score = len(matched) / len(keywords)\n    return score, matched\n\n\ndef category_match_patterns() -> Dict[str, List[Tuple[str, float]]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Return patterns that indicate specific failure categories.\n    Each pattern has a base score weight.\n    \"\"\"\n    return {\n        'OPEN': [\n            (r'开路|open|OS.*open|OS，像开路', 0.9),\n            (r'虚焊|cold.?solder|少锡', 0.7),\n        ],\n        'SHORT': [\n            (r'短路|short|桥连|连锡|solder.?bridge', 0.9),\n            (r'OS.*short|OS，像短路', 0.85),\n        ],\n        'COLD_SOLDER': [\n            (r'虚焊|cold.?solder|浮高|intermittent', 0.9),\n            (r'功能不稳', 0.7),\n        ],\n        'FIXTURE_CONTACT': [\n            (r'接触不良|fixture.?contact|重插后偶尔OK|reseat', 0.95),\n            (r'治具|探针|pogo', 0.8),\n        ],\n        'PROBE_WORN': [\n            (r'探针磨损|probe.?worn|换针|replace.?pin', 0.95),\n        ],\n        'FLASH_FAIL': [\n            (r'烧录失败|写入失败|program.*fail|flash.*fail|重烧不稳定', 0.95),\n        ],\n        'CHECKSUM_FAIL': [\n            (r'校验失败|CRC|checksum|verify.?fail', 0.95),\n        ],\n        'BOOT_FAIL': [\n            (r'启动.*失败|boot.*fail|OS.?fail|cannot.?boot|卡logo|进不了系统', 0.95),\n        ],\n        'VERSION_MISMATCH': [\n            (r'版本不符|下错程序|version.?mismatch|wrong.?FW', 0.95),\n        ],\n        'LEAKAGE': [\n            (r'漏电|leakage|HiPot|hipot|绝缘不良', 0.95),\n        ],\n        'CURRENT_HIGH': [\n            (r'静态电流.*高|idle.?current.*high|功耗高|电流偏高', 0.95),\n            (r'短路趋势|器件异常', 0.7),\n        ],\n        'CAL_DATA_FAIL': [\n            (r'校准.*失败|cal.*fail|RF.*校准|重校仍挂', 0.95),\n        ],\n        'CONTAMINATION': [\n            (r'污染|助焊剂|残留|contamination|residue', 0.9),\n        ],\n        'REVERSED': [\n            (r'反向|极性|polarity|reversed|装反', 0.95),\n        ],\n        'PRESSURE_MARK': [\n            (r'压伤|pressure.?mark|pad.?damage', 0.95),\n        ],\n        'ESD': [\n            (r'ESD|静电', 0.95),\n        ],\n        'MISSING': [\n            (r'少件|missing', 0.95),\n        ],\n        'CONNECTOR_WEAR': [\n            (r'connector.?wear|插拔|insertion.?loss', 0.9),\n        ],\n    }\n\n\ndef extract_component_reference(text: str) -> Optional[str]:\n    \"\"\"Extract component reference from text (e.g., R152, U12, C87).\"\"\"\n    # Pattern for component references\n    patterns = [\n        r'\\b([RUCDQLJ]\\d+)\\b',           # R152, U12, C87, D34, Q15, L5, J3\n        r'\\b(TP\\d+)\\b',                   # TP5\n        r'\\b(ANT\\d*)\\b',                  # ANT1\n        r'\\b(SH\\d+)\\b',                   # SH1\n        r'\\b([A-Z]\\d+_[A-Z0-9]+)\\b',      # U12_VDD style\n    ]\n\n    for pattern in patterns:\n        match = re.search(pattern, text, re.IGNORECASE)\n        if match:\n            return match.group(1).upper()\n    return None\n\n\ndef extract_net_reference(text: str) -> Optional[str]:\n    \"\"\"Extract net/signal reference from text.\"\"\"\n    net_patterns = [\n        r'\\b(I2C_SDA|I2C_SCL|SPI_MISO|SPI_MOSI|RESET_N|PGOOD|SW_NODE|VBUS_SNS|VDD_\\w+)\\b',\n        r'\\b(R152_NET|U12_VDD|ANT_FEED|RF_IN|RF_OUT)\\b',\n        r'\\b(JTAG_CHAIN|BOUNDARY_\\w+|SCAN_IDCODE)\\b',\n        r'\\b(FLASH_Program|HIPOT_Leakage|RF_\\w+)\\b',\n        r'\\b(Thermal_Trip|Brownout_Reset|Idle_Current)\\b',\n    ]\n\n    for pattern in net_patterns:\n        match = re.search(pattern, text, re.IGNORECASE)\n        if match:\n            return match.group(1)\n    return None\n\n\ndef score_candidate(\n    segment: Segment,\n    entry: CodebookEntry,\n    record: LogRecord,\n    category_patterns: Dict[str, List[Tuple[str, float]]]\n) -> Tuple[float, str]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Score how well a codebook entry matches a segment.\n    Returns (score, rationale).\n    \"\"\"\n    text = segment.span_text\n    score = 0.0\n    evidence = []\n\n    # 1. Keyword matching (weight: 0.35)\n    kw_score, matched_kws = keyword_match_score(text, entry.keywords)\n    score += kw_score * 0.35\n    if matched_kws:\n        evidence.append(f\"keywords: {', '.join(matched_kws[:3])}\")\n\n    # 2. Category pattern matching (weight: 0.25)\n    category_key = entry.category_lv2\n    if category_key in category_patterns:\n        patterns = category_patterns[category_key]\n        best_pattern_score = 0.0\n        for pattern, weight in patterns:\n            if re.search(pattern, text, re.IGNORECASE):\n                best_pattern_score = max(best_pattern_score, weight)\n        score += best_pattern_score * 0.25\n        if best_pattern_score > 0:\n            evidence.append(f\"category={entry.category_lv2}\")\n\n    # 3. Standard label similarity (weight: 0.20)\n    label_score = fuzzy_match_score(text, entry.standard_label)\n    score += label_score * 0.20\n\n    # 4. Station compatibility (weight: 0.10)\n    if entry.station_scope:\n        if record.station in entry.station_scope:\n            score += 0.10\n            evidence.append(f\"station={record.station}\")\n        else:\n            # Penalty for station mismatch\n            score -= 0.05\n\n    # 5. Component/Net reference alignment (weight: 0.10)\n    segment_component = extract_component_reference(text)\n    segment_net = extract_net_reference(text)\n    label_component = extract_component_reference(entry.standard_label)\n    label_net = extract_net_reference(entry.standard_label)\n\n    if segment_component and label_component:\n        if segment_component == label_component:\n            score += 0.10\n            evidence.append(f\"component={segment_component}\")\n        else:\n            score -= 0.02\n\n    if segment_net and label_net:\n        if segment_net.upper() == label_net.upper():\n            score += 0.08\n\n    # 6. Fail code alignment (bonus)\n    if record.fail_code:\n        # Check if fail code indicates specific failure type\n        fail_code_prefix = record.fail_code[0] if record.fail_code else ''\n        if fail_code_prefix == 'E' and entry.category_lv1 == 'ELECTRICAL':\n            score += 0.03\n        elif fail_code_prefix == 'F' and entry.category_lv1 == 'FW_SW':\n            score += 0.03\n        elif fail_code_prefix == 'H' and 'HIPOT' in entry.standard_label.upper():\n            score += 0.03\n        elif fail_code_prefix == 'B' and 'BURNIN' in str(entry.station_scope):\n            score += 0.03\n        elif fail_code_prefix == 'S' and 'BST' in str(entry.station_scope):\n            score += 0.03\n        elif fail_code_prefix == 'R' and ('RF' in entry.standard_label or 'CAL' in entry.category_lv2):\n            score += 0.03\n\n        if record.fail_code:\n            evidence.append(f\"fail_code={record.fail_code}\")\n\n    # 7. Test item alignment\n    if record.test_item:\n        if record.test_item in entry.standard_label or record.test_item in text:\n            score += 0.02\n            evidence.append(f\"test_item={record.test_item}\")\n\n    # Normalize score to [0, 1]\n    score = max(0.0, min(1.0, score))\n\n    rationale = \"; \".join(evidence) if evidence else \"generic match\"\n\n    return score, rationale\n\n\ndef deterministic_tiebreak(\n    candidates: List[Tuple[float, CodebookEntry, str]],\n    record: LogRecord,\n    segment: Segment,\n    margin: float = 0.05\n) -> Tuple[CodebookEntry, str]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Deterministic tie-breaking when multiple candidates are within margin of best score.\n    Uses record context to make reproducible choices.\n    \"\"\"\n    if not candidates:\n        return None, \"\"\n\n    best_score = candidates[0][0]\n    near_best = [(score, entry, rationale) for score, entry, rationale in candidates\n                 if best_score - score <= margin]\n\n    if len(near_best) == 1:\n        return near_best[0][1], near_best[0][2]\n\n    # Create a deterministic hash from context\n    context_str = f\"{record.record_id}:{segment.segment_id}:{record.station}:{record.fail_code}:{record.test_item}\"\n    hash_val = int(hashlib.md5(context_str.encode()).hexdigest(), 16)\n\n    # Select based on hash\n    selected_idx = hash_val % len(near_best)\n    return near_best[selected_idx][1], near_best[selected_idx][2]\n\n\ndef calibrate_confidence(\n    match_score: float,\n    is_unknown: bool,\n    record: LogRecord,\n    segment: Segment\n) -> float:\n    \"\"\"\n    Calibrate confidence from match score.\n    UNKNOWN predictions should generally be less confident than non-UNKNOWN.\n    \"\"\"\n    if is_unknown:\n        # UNKNOWN confidence: lower range [0.15, 0.45]\n        # Scale based on how close to threshold\n        base_conf = 0.15 + (match_score * 0.30)\n        # Add small deterministic variation\n        context_hash = hash(f\"{record.record_id}:{segment.segment_id}\")\n        variation = (context_hash % 100) / 1000  # 0.000 to 0.099\n        confidence = base_conf + (variation * 0.15)\n        confidence = min(0.45, max(0.15, confidence))\n    else:\n        # Non-UNKNOWN confidence: higher range [0.50, 0.98]\n        # Scale based on match quality\n        if match_score >= 0.8:\n            base_conf = 0.85 + (match_score - 0.8) * 0.65  # 0.85-0.98\n        elif match_score >= 0.6:\n            base_conf = 0.65 + (match_score - 0.6) * 1.0   # 0.65-0.85\n        elif match_score >= 0.4:\n            base_conf = 0.50 + (match_score - 0.4) * 0.75  # 0.50-0.65\n        else:\n            base_conf = 0.50\n\n        # Add deterministic variation for diversity\n        context_hash = hash(f\"{record.record_id}:{segment.segment_id}\")\n        variation = (context_hash % 100) / 500  # 0.00 to 0.198\n        confidence = base_conf + (variation * 0.10) - 0.05\n        confidence = min(0.98, max(0.50, confidence))\n\n    return round(confidence, 4)\n\n\ndef normalize_segment(\n    segment: Segment,\n    record: LogRecord,\n    codebook: List[CodebookEntry],\n    category_patterns: Dict[str, List[Tuple[str, float]]],\n    unknown_threshold: float = 0.30\n) -> PredictionResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Normalize a single segment to a codebook entry.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Filter candidates by station scope\n    valid_candidates = []\n    for entry in codebook:\n        if entry.station_scope and record.station not in entry.station_scope:\n            continue\n        score, rationale = score_candidate(segment, entry, record, category_patterns)\n        valid_candidates.append((score, entry, rationale))\n\n    # Sort by score descending\n    valid_candidates.sort(key=lambda x: -x[0])\n\n    if not valid_candidates or valid_candidates[0][0] < unknown_threshold:\n        # UNKNOWN case\n        confidence = calibrate_confidence(\n            valid_candidates[0][0] if valid_candidates else 0.0,\n            is_unknown=True,\n            record=record,\n            segment=segment\n        )\n        rationale = f\"weak evidence (score={valid_candidates[0][0]:.2f})\" if valid_candidates else \"no candidates\"\n        return PredictionResult(\n            segment_id=segment.segment_id,\n            span_text=segment.span_text,\n            pred_code=\"UNKNOWN\",\n            pred_label=\"\",\n            confidence=confidence,\n            rationale=rationale\n        )\n\n    # Apply tie-breaking if needed\n    best_entry, best_rationale = deterministic_tiebreak(valid_candidates, record, segment)\n    best_score = valid_candidates[0][0]\n\n    confidence = calibrate_confidence(best_score, is_unknown=False, record=record, segment=segment)\n\n    return PredictionResult(\n        segment_id=segment.segment_id,\n        span_text=segment.span_text,\n        pred_code=best_entry.code,\n        pred_label=best_entry.standard_label,\n        confidence=confidence,\n        rationale=best_rationale\n    )\n\n\ndef process_records(\n    records: List[LogRecord],\n    codebooks: Dict[str, List[CodebookEntry]]\n) -> List[dict]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Process all records and return normalized results.\n    \"\"\"\n    category_patterns = category_match_patterns()\n    results = []\n\n    for record in records:\n        # Get codebook for this product\n        codebook = codebooks.get(record.product_id, [])\n        if not codebook:\n            print(f\"Warning: No codebook found for product {record.product_id}\")\n            continue\n\n        # Segment the raw reason text\n        segments = segment_reason_text(record.raw_reason_text, record.record_id)\n\n        # Normalize each segment\n        normalized = []\n        for segment in segments:\n            prediction = normalize_segment(segment, record, codebook, category_patterns)\n            normalized.append({\n                \"segment_id\": prediction.segment_id,\n                \"span_text\": prediction.span_text,\n                \"pred_code\": prediction.pred_code,\n                \"pred_label\": prediction.pred_label,\n                \"confidence\": prediction.confidence,\n                \"rationale\": prediction.rationale\n            })\n\n        # text2.lower().strip()\n    if t1 == t2:\n        return 1.0\n    ratio = SequenceMatcher(None, t1, t2).ratio()\n    return ratio\n\n\ndef keyword_match_score(text: str, keywords: List[str]) -> Tuple[float, List[str]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate how many keywords match the text.\n    Returns score and list of matched keywords.\n    \"\"\"\n    text_lower = text.lower()\n    matched = []\n\n    for kw in keywords:\n        kw_lower = kw.lower().strip()\n        if kw_lower and kw_lower in text_lower:\n            matched.append(kw)\n\n    if not keywords:\n        return 0.0, []\n\n    # Weight longer matches higher\n    weighted_score = sum(len(m) for m in matched) / sum(len(k) for k in keywords) if keywords else 0\n    count_score = len(matched) / len(keywords)\n    score = 0.6 * weighted_score + 0.4 * count_score\n\n    return min(1.0, score), matched\n\n\ndef category_match_patterns() -> Dict[str, List[Tuple[str, float]]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Return patterns that indicate (r'connector.?wear|插拔|insertion.?loss', 0.9),\n        ],\n    }\n\n\ndef extract_component_reference(text: str) -> Optional[str]:\n    \"\"\"Extract component reference from text (e.g., R152, U12, C87).\"\"\"\n    patterns = [\n        r'\\b([RUCDQLJ]\\d+)\\b',\n        r'\\b(TP\\d+)\\b',\n        r'\\b(ANT\\d*)\\b',\n        r'\\b(SH\\d+)\\b',\n    ]\n    for pattern in patterns:\n \"\"\"Extract net/signal reference from text.\"\"\"\n    net_patterns = [\n        r'\\b(I2C_SDA|I2C_SCL|SPI_MISO|SPI_MOSI|RESET_N|PGOOD|SW_NODE|VBUS_SNS)\\b',\n LogRecord,\n    category_patterns: Dict[str, List[Tuple[str, float]]]\n) -> Tuple[float, str]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Score how well a codebook entry matches a segment.\n    Returns (score, rationale).\n    \"\"\"\n    text = segment.span_text\n    score = 0.0\n    evidence = []\n\n    # 1. Keyword matching (weight: 0.40)\n    kw_score, matched_kws = keyword_match_score(text, entry.keywords)\n    kw_contribution = kw_score * 0.40\n    score += kw_contribution\n    if matched_kws:\n        evidence.append(f\"keywords: {', '.join(matched_kws[:3])}\")\n\n    # 2. Category pattern matching (weight: 0.30)\n    category_key = entry.category_lv2\n    best_pattern_score = 0.0\n    if category_key in category_patterns:\n        patterns = category_patterns[category_key]\n        for pattern, weight in patterns:\n            if re.search(pattern, text, re.IGNORECASE):\n                best_pattern_score = max(best_pattern_score, weight)\n    score += best_pattern_score * 0.30\n    if best_pattern_score > 0:\n        evidence.append(f\"category={entry.category_lv2}\")\n\n    # 3. Standard label similarity (weight: 0.15)\n    label_score = fuzzy_match_score(text, entry.standard_label)\n    score += label_score * 0.15\n\n    # 4. Station compatibility (weight: 0.10)\n    if entry.station_scope:\n        if record.station in entry.station_scope:\n            score += 0.10\n            evidence.append(f\"station={record.station}\")\n        else:\n            score -= 0.08  # Penalty for station mismatch\n\n    # 5. Component/Net reference alignment (weight: 0.05)\n    segment_net = extract_net_reference(text)\n    label_net = extract_net_reference(entry.standard_label)\n\n    if segment_net and label_net:\n        if segment_net.upper() == label_net.upper():\n            score += 0.05\n\n    # 6. Fail code alignment (bonus up to 0.05)\n    if record.fail_code:\n        fail_code_prefix = record.fail_code[0] if record.fail_code else ''\n        bonus = 0.0\n        if fail_code_prefix == 'E' and entry.category_lv1 == 'ELECTRICAL':\n            bonus = 0.03\n        elif fail_code_prefix == 'F' and entry.category_lv1 == 'FW_SW':\n            bonus = 0.04\n        elif fail_code_prefix == 'H' and entry.category_lv2 == 'LEAKAGE':\n            bonus = 0.04\n        elif fail_code_prefix == 'B' and entry.category_lv1 in ['ELECTRICAL', 'SOLDER']:\n            bonus = 0.02\n        elif fail_code_prefix == 'S' and entry.category_lv1 in ['TEST_SYSTEM', 'ELECTRICAL']:\n            bonus = 0.02\n        elif fail_code_prefix == 'R' and ('RF' in entry.category_lv2 or 'CAL' in entry.category_lv2):\n            bonus = 0.04\n\n        score += bonus\n        if record.fail_code:\n            evidence.append(f\"fail_code={record.fail_code}\")\n\n    # 7. Test item alignment\n    if record.test_item and record.test_item in entry.standard_label:\n        score += 0.03\n        evidence.append(f\"test_item={record.test_item}\")\n\n    # Normalize score to [0, 1]\n    score = max(0.0, min(1.0, score))\n\n    rationale = \"; \".join(evidence) if evidence else \"generic match\"\n\n    return score, rationale\n\n\ndef deterministic_tiebreak(\n    candidates: List[Tuple[float, CodebookEntry, str]],\n    record: LogRecord,\n    segment: Segment,\n    margin: float = 0.03\n) -> Tuple[CodebookEntry, str, float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Deterministic tie-breaking when multiple candidates are within margin of best score.\n    Returns (best_entry, rationale, score).\n    \"\"\"\n    if not candidates:\n        return None, \"\", 0.0\n\n    best_score = candidates[0][0]\n    near_best = [(score, entry, rationale) for score, entry, rationale in candidates\n                 if best_score - score <= margin]\n\n    if len(near_best) == 1:\n        return near_best[0][1], near_best[0][2], near_best[0][0]\n\n    # Create a deterministic hash from context\n    context_str = f\"{record.record_id}:{segment.segment_id}:{record.station}:{record.fail_code}:{record.test_item}\"\n    hash_val = int(hashlib.md5(context_str.encode()).hexdigest(), 16)\n\n    selected_idx = hash_val % len(near_best)\n    return near_best[selected_idx][1], near_best[selected_idx][2], near_best[selected_idx][0]\n\n\ndef calibrate_confidence(\n    match_score: float,\n    is_unknown: bool,\n    record: LogRecord,\n    segment: Segment\n) -> float:\n    \"\"\"\n    Calibrate confidence from match score with more diversity.\n    UNKNOWN predictions should generally be less confident than non-UNKNOWN.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Create deterministic variation\n    context_str = f\"{record.record_id}:{segment.segment_id}\"\n    hash_val = int(hashlib.md5(context_str.encode()).hexdigest(), 16)\n    variation = (hash_val % 1000) / 10000  # 0.0 to 0.0999\n\n    if is_unknown:\n        # UNKNOWN confidence: range [0.10, 0.48]\n        # Lower scores get lower confidence\n        if match_score < 0.15:\n            base_conf = 0.10 + match_score * 0.8  # 0.10 - 0.22\n        elif match_score < 0.25:\n            base_conf = 0.22 + (match_score - 0.15) * 1.5  # 0.22 - 0.37\n        else:\n            base_conf = 0.37 + (match_score - 0.25) * 2.0  # 0.37 - 0.47\n\n        # Add variation\n        confidence = base_conf + variation * 0.08 - 0.02\n        confidence = round(max(0.10, min(0.48, confidence)), 4)\n    else:\n        # Non-UNKNOWN confidence: range [0.52, 0.98]\n        # Higher scores get higher confidence\n        if match_score >= 0.75:\n            base_conf = 0.88 + (match_score - 0.75) * 0.4  # 0.88 - 0.98\n        elif match_score >= 0.55:\n            base_conf = 0.72 + (match_score - 0.55) * 0.8  # 0.72 - 0.88\n        elif match_score >= 0.40:\n            base_conf = 0.58 + (match_score - 0.40) * 0.93  # 0.58 - 0.72\n        else:\n            base_conf = 0.52 + (match_score - 0.30) * 0.6  # 0.52 - 0.58\n\n        # Add variation\n        confidence = base_conf + variation * 0.06 - 0.03\n        confidence = round(max(0.52, min(0.98, confidence)), 4)\n\n    return confidence\n\n\ndef normalize_segment(\n    segment: Segment,\n    record: LogRecord,\n    codebook: List[CodebookEntry],\n    category_patterns: Dict[str, List[Tuple[str, float]]],\n    unknown_threshold: float = 0.28\n) -> PredictionResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Normalize a single segment to a codebook entry.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Filter candidates by station scope\n    valid_candidates = []\n    for entry in codebook:\n        if entry.station_scope and re.IGNORECASE):\n   143→            return True\n   144→    return False\n   145→\n   146→\n   147→def segment_reason_text(raw_text: str, record_id: str) -> List[Segment]:\n   148→    \"\"\"\n   149→    Segment raw_reason_text into multiple segments based on delimiters.\n   150→    Keeps logical units together (e.g., Chinese phrases).\n   151→    \"\"\"\n   152→    if not raw_text or raw_text.strip() == '':\n   153→        return []\n   154→\n   155→    # Pre-process: normalize some variations\n   156→    text = raw_text.strip()\n   157→\n   158→    # Keep certain phrases together '探针磨损导致接触差，建议换针': '<<PROBE_WORN_CN>>',\n   165→        'probe worn causing bad contact, replace pin': '<<PROBE_WORN_EN>>',\n   166→        '静态电流偏高，疑似短路趋势/器件异常': '<<CURRENT_HIGH_CN>>',\n   167→        'idle current high, suspect partial short/component issue': '<<CURRENT_HIGH_EN>>',\n   168→        '烧录失败/写入失败，重烧不稳定': '<<FLASH_FAIL_CN>>',\n   169→        'program/flash write failed, reflash failed, recal still NG': '<<RF_CAL_FAIL_EN>>',\n   178→        '开机启动失败，卡logo/进不了系统': '<<BOOT_FAIL_CN>>',\n   179→        'boot failed, stuck at logo / cannot boot': '<<BOOT_FAIL_EN>>',\n   180→        'OS fail，启动不了': '<<OS_FAIL_CN>>',\n   181→        'OS fail, cannot boot': '<<OS_FAIL_EN>>',\n   182→        'BST 异常，需确认原因': '<<BST_ABNORMAL_CN>>',\n   183→        'BST abnormal, need check': '<<BST_ABNORMAL_EN>>',\n   184→        '疑似助焊剂残留/污染影响测试': '<<CONTAMINATION_CN>>',\n   185→        '疑似ESD损伤，需复测确认': '<<ESD_CN>>',\n   186→        '极性反向/装反，需返修': '<<REVERSED_CN>>',\n   187→        'polarity reversed, rework needed': '<<REVERSED_EN>>',\n   188→        'fixture pressure mark, pad/trace damage': '<<PRESSURE_MARK_EN>>',\n   189→    }\n   190→\n   191→    for phrase, placeholder in phrase_replacements.items():\n   192→        text = text.replace(phrase, placeholder)\n   193→\n   194→    # Primary segment delimiters (stronger breaks)\n   195→    primary_delimiters = r'\\s*/\\s*|\\s*;\\s*|\\s*；\\s*|\\s*&\\s*|\\s*\\+\\s*'\n   196→\n   197→    # Split by primary delimiters\n   198→    parts = re.split(primary_delimiters, text)\n   199→\n   200→    # Further split by Chinese comma followed by non-digit (avoid splitting measurements)\n   201→    final_parts = []\n   202→    for part in parts:\n   203→        # Split by Chinese enumeration comma or comma\n   204→        sub_parts = re.split(r'\\s*、\\s*|\\s*，\\s*(?=[^\\d])', part)\n   205→        final_parts.extend(sub_parts)\n   206→\n   207→    # Restore placeholders and clean up\n   208→    restore_map = {v: k for k, v in phrase_replacements.items()}\n   209→\n   210→    # Noise words to strip from end (but not middle)\n   211→    noise_suffixes = ['NG', 'OK了', '已处理', '浮高', '挂', '有问题', '可能', '疑似',\n   212→                      '接触不好', '不稳定', '确认', '返修', '换掉', '复测', '重测过',\n   213→                      'rework', 'retest', 'suspect', 'intermittent', '建议', '按流程',\n   214→                      'suspect', 'then OK', '换掉']\n   215→\n   216→    segments = []\n   217→    search_pos = 0\n   218→\n   219→    for part in final_parts:\n   220→        part = part.strip()\n   221→        if not part:\n   222→            continue\n   223→\n   224→        # Restore any placeholders\n   225→        for placeholder, original in restore_map.items():\n   226→            part = part.replace(placeholder, original)\n   227→\n   228→        # Skip if just noise or too short\n   229→        if len(part) < 3:\n   230→            continue\n   231→\n   232→        # Skip station info segments\n   233→        if is_station_info_segment(part):\n   234→            continue\n   235→\n   236→        # Clean noise suffixes\n   237→        clean_part = part\n   238→        for noise in noise_suffixes:\n   239→            if clean_part.endswith(' ' + noise):\n   240→                clean_part = clean_part[:-len(' ' + noise)].strip()\n   241→            elif clean_part.endswith(noise) and len(clean_part) > len(noise) + 2:\n   242→                # Only strip if there's substantial content before\n   243→                clean_part = clean_part[:-len(noise)].strip()\n   244→\n   245→        if len(clean_part) < 3:\n   246→            continue\n   247→\n   248→        # Find position in original text\n   249→        start_idx = raw_text.find(part, search_pos)\n   250→        if start_idx == -1:\n   251→            # Try finding the cleaned version\n   252→            start_idx = raw_text.find(clean_part, search_pos)\n   253→            if start_idx == -1:\n   254→                start_idx = search_pos\n   255→\n   256→        end_idx = start_idx + len(part)\n   257→        search_pos = max(search_pos, end_idx)\n   258→\n   259→        segment_id = f\"{record_id}-S{len(segments) + 1}\"\n   260→        segments.append(Segment(\n   261→            segment_id=segment_id,\n   262→            span_text=part,\n   263→            start_idx=start_idx,\n   264→            end_idx=end_idx\n   265→        ))\n   266→\n   267→    # If no segments found, treat the whole text as one segment\n   268→    if not segments and len(raw_text.strip()) >= 3:\n   269→        segments.append(Segment(\n   270→            segment_id=f\"{record_id}-S1\",\n   271→            span_text=raw_text.strip(),\n   272→            start_idx=0,\n   273→            end_idx=len(raw_text)\n   274→        ))\n   275→\n   276→    return segments\n   277→\n   278→\n   279→def fuzzy_match_score(text1: str, text2: str) -> float:\n   280→    \"\"\"Calculate fuzzy string matching score between two texts.\"\"\"\n   281→    t1 = text1.lower().strip()\n   282→    t2 = text2.lower().strip()\n   283→    if t1 == t2:\n   284→        return 1.0\n   285→    ratio = SequenceMatcher(None, t1, t2).ratio()\n   286→    return ratio\n   287→\n   288→\n   289→def keyword_match_score(text: str, keywords: List[str]) -> Tuple[float, List[str]]:\n   290→    \"\"\"\n   291→    Calculate how many keywords match the text.\n   292→    Returns score and list of matched keywords.\n   293→    \"\"\"\n   294→    text_lower = text.lower()\n   295→    matched = []\n   296→\n   297→    for kw in keywords:\n   298→        kw_lower = kw.lower().strip()\n   299→        if kw_lower and kw_lower in text_lower:\n   300→            matched.append(kw)\n   301→\n   302→    if not keywords:\n   303→        return 0.0, []\n   304→\n   305→    # Weight longer matches higher\n   306→    weighted_score = sum(len(m) for m in matched) / sum(len(k) for k in keywords) if keywords else 0\n   307→    count_score = len(matched) / len(keywords)\n   308→    score = 0.6 * weighted_score + 0.4 * count_score\n   309→\n   310→    return min(1.0, score), matched\n   311→\n   312→\n   313→def category_match_patterns() -> Dict[str, List[Tuple[str, float]]]:\n   314→    \"\"\"\n   315→    Return patterns that indicate specific failure categories.\n   316→    Each pattern has a base score weight.\n   317→    \"\"\"\n   318→    return {\n   319→        'OPEN': [\n   320→            (r'开路|open(?!\\s*circuit)|OS.*open|OS，像开路', 0.85),\n   321→            (r'虚焊|cold.?solder|少锡', 0.5),\n   322→        ],\n   323→        'SHORT': [\n   324→            (r'短路|short|桥连|连锡|solder.?bridge', 0.9),\n   325→            (r'OS.*short|OS，像短路', 0.85),\n   326→        ],\n   327→        'COLD_SOLDER': [\n   328→            (r'虚焊|cold.?solder|浮高|intermittent', 0.9),\n   329→            (r'功能不稳', 0.75),\n   330→        ],\n   331→        'FIXTURE_CONTACT': [\n   332→            (r'接触不良|fixture.?contact|重插后偶尔OK|reseat', 0.95),\n   333→            (r'治具|探针|pogo', 0.75),\n   334→        ],\n   335→        'PROBE_WORN': [\n   336→            (r'探针磨损|probe.?worn|换针|replace.?pin', 0.95),\n   337→        ],\n   338→        'FLASH_FAIL': [\n   339→            (r'烧录失败|写入失败|program.*fail|flash.*fail|重烧不稳定', 0.95),\n   340→        ],\n   341→        'CHECKSUM_FAIL': [\n   342→            (r'校验失败|CRC|checksum|verify.?fail', 0.95),\n   343→        ],\n   344→        'BOOT_FAIL': [\n   345→            (r'启动.*失败|boot.*fail|OS\\s*fail|cannot.?boot|卡logo|进不了系统|启动不了', 0.95),\n   346→        ],\n   347→        'VERSION_MISMATCH': [\n   348→            (r'版本不符|下错程序|version.?mismatch|wrong.?FW', 0.95),\n   349→        ],\n   350→        'LEAKAGE': [\n   351→            (r'漏电|漏點|leakage|HiPot|hipot|绝缘不良', 0.95),\n   352→        ],\n   353→        'CURRENT_HIGH': [\n   354→            (r'静态电流.*高|idle.?current.*high|功耗高|电流偏高', 0.95),\n   355→            (r'短路趋势|器件异常', 0.6),\n   356→        ],\n   357→        'CAL_DATA_FAIL': [\n   358→            (r'校准.*失败|cal.*fail|RF.*校准|重校仍挂|校准数据', 0.95),\n   359→        ],\n   360→        'CONTAMINATION': [\n   361→            (r'污染|助焊剂|残留|contamination|residue', 0.9),\n   362→        ],\n   363→        'REVERSED': [\n   364→            (r'反向|极性|polarity|reversed|装反', 0.95),\n   365→        ],\n   366→        'PRESSURE_MARK': [\n   367→            (r'压伤|pressure.?mark|pad.?damage', 0.95),\n   368→        ],\n   369→        'ESD': [\n   370→            (r'ESD|静电损伤', 0.95),\n   371→        ],\n   372→        'MISSING': [\n   373→            (r'少件|missing', 0.95),\n   374→        ],\n   375→        'CONNECTOR_WEAR': [\n   376→            (r'connector.?wear|插拔|insertion.?loss', 0.9),\n   377→        ],\n   378→    }\n   379→\n   380→\n   381→def extract_component_reference(text: str) -> Optional[str]:\n   382→    \"\"\"Extract component reference from text (e.g., R152, U12, C87).\"\"\"\n   383→    patterns = [\n   384→        r'\\b([RUCDQLJ]\\d+)\\b',\n   385→        r'\\b(TP\\d+)\\b',\n   386→        r'\\b(ANT\\d*)\\b',\n   387→        r'\\b(SH\\d+)\\b',\n   388→    ]\n   389→    for pattern in patterns:\n   390→        match = re.search(pattern, text, re.IGNORECASE)\n   391→        if match:\n   392→            return match.group(1).upper()\n   393→    return None\n   394→\n   395→\n   396→def extract_net_reference(text: str) -> Optional[str]:\n   397→    \"\"\"Extract net/signal reference from text.\"\"\"\n   398→    net_patterns = [\n   399→        r'\\b(I2C_SDA|I2C_SCL|SPI_MISO|SPI_MOSI|RESET_N|PGOOD|SW_NODE|VBUS_SNS)\\b',\n   400→        r'\\b(R152_NET|U12_VDD|ANT_FEED|RF_IN|RF_OUT|VDD_\\w+)\\b',\n   401→        r'\\b(JTAG_CHAIN|BOUNDARY_OPEN|BOUNDARY_SHORT|SCAN_IDCODE)\\b',\n   402→        r'\\b(FLASH_Program|HIPOT_Leakage)\\b',\n   403→        r'\\b(RF_TX_PWR|RF_RX_SENS|RF_PLL_LOCK|RF_CAL_WRITE)\\b',\n   404→        r'\\b(Thermal_Trip|Brownout_Reset|Idle_Current|Reset_Loop)\\b',\n   405→    ]\n   406→    for pattern in net_patterns:\n   407→        match = re.search(pattern, text, re.IGNORECASE)\n   408→        if match:\n   409→            return match.group(1)\n   410→    return None\n   411→\n   412→\n   413→def score_candidate(\n   414→    segment: Segment,\n   415→    entry: CodebookEntry,\n   416→    record: LogRecord,\n   417→    category_patterns: Dict[str, List[Tuple[str, float]]]\n   418→) -> Tuple[float, str]:\n   419→    \"\"\"\n   420→    Score how well a codebook entry matches a segment.\n   421→    Returns (score, rationale).\n   422→    \"\"\"\n   423→    text = segment.span_text\n   424→    score = 0.0\n   425→    evidence = []\n   426→\n   427→    # 1. Keyword matching (weight: 0.40)\n   428→    kw_score, matched_kws = keyword_match_score(text, entry.keywords)\n   429→    kw_contribution = kw_score * 0.40\n   430→    score += kw_contribution\n   431→    if matched_kws:\n   432→        evidence.append(f\"keywords: {', '.join(matched_kws[:3])}\")\n   433→\n   434→    # 2. Category pattern matching (weight: 0.30)\n   435→    category_key = entry.category_lv2\n   436→    best_pattern_score = 0.0\n   437→    if category_key in category_patterns:\n   438→        patterns = category_patterns[category_key]\n   439→        for pattern, weight in patterns:\n   440→            if re.search(pattern, text, re.IGNORECASE):\n   441→                best_pattern_score = max(best_pattern_score, weight)\n   442→    score += best_pattern_score * 0.30\n   443→    if best_pattern_score > 0:\n   444→        evidence.append(f\"category={entry.category_lv2}\")\n   445→\n   446→    # 3. Standard label similarity (weight: 0.15)\n   447→    label_score = fuzzy_match_score(text, entry.standard_label)\n   448→    score += label_score * 0.15\n   449→\n   450→    # 4. Station compatibility (weight: 0.10)\n   451→    if entry.station_scope:\n   452→        if record.station in entry.station_scope:\n   453→            score += 0.10\n   454→            evidence.append(f\"station={record.station}\")\n   455→        else:\n   456→            score -= 0.08  # Penalty for station mismatch\n   457→\n   458→    # 5. Component/Net reference alignment (weight: 0.05)\n   459→    segment_net = extract_net_reference(text)\n   460→    label_net = extract_net_reference(entry.standard_label)\n   461→\n   462→    if segment_net and label_net:\n   463→        if segment_net.upper() == label_net.upper():\n   464→            score += 0.05\n   465→\n   466→    # 6. Fail code alignment (bonus up to 0.05)\n   467→    if record.fail_code:\n   468→        fail_code_prefix = record.fail_code[0] if record.fail_code else ''\n   469→        bonus = 0.0\n   470→        if fail_code_prefix == 'E' and entry.category_lv1 == 'ELECTRICAL':\n   471→            bonus = 0.03\n   472→        elif fail_code_prefix == 'F' and entry.category_lv1 == 'FW_SW':\n   473→            bonus = 0.04\n   474→        elif fail_code_prefix == 'H' and entry.category_lv2 == 'LEAKAGE':\n   475→            bonus = 0.04\n   476→        elif fail_code_prefix == 'B' and entry.category_lv1 in ['ELECTRICAL', 'SOLDER']:\n   477→            bonus = 0.02\n   478→        elif fail_code_prefix == 'S' and entry.category_lv1 in ['TEST_SYSTEM', 'ELECTRICAL']:\n   479→            bonus = 0.02\n   480→        elif fail_code_prefix == 'R' and ('RF' in entry.category_lv2 or 'CAL' in entry.category_lv2):\n   481→            bonus = 0.04\n   482→\n   483→        score += bonus\n   484→        if record.fail_code:\n   485→            evidence.append(f\"fail_code={record.fail_code}\")\n   486→\n   487→    # 7. Test item alignment\n   488→    if record.test_item and record.test_item in entry.standard_label:\n   489→        score += 0.03\n   490→        evidence.append(f\"test_item={record.test_item}\")\n   491→\n   492→    # Normalize score to [0, 1]\n   493→    score = max(0.0, min(1.0, score))\n   494→\n   495→    rationale = \"; \".join(evidence) if evidence else \"generic match\"\n   496→\n   497→    return score, rationale\n   498→\n   499→\n   500→def deterministic_tiebreak(\n   501→    candidates: List[Tuple[float, CodebookEntry, str]],\n   502→    record: LogRecord,\n   503→    segment: Segment,\n   504→    margin: float = 0.03\n   505→) -> Tuple[CodebookEntry, str, float]:\n   506→    \"\"\"\n   507→    Deterministic tie-breaking when multiple candidates are within margin of best score.\n   508→    Returns (best_entry, rationale, score).\n   509→    \"\"\"\n   510→    if not candidates:\n   511→        return None, \"\", 0.0\n   512→\n   513→    best_score = candidates[0][0]\n   514→    near_best = [(score, entry, rationale) for score, entry, rationale in candidates\n   515→                 if best_score - score <= margin]\n   516→\n   517→    if len(near_best) == 1:\n   518→        return near_best[0][1], near_best[0][2], near_best[0][0]\n   519→\n   520→    # Create a deterministic hash from context\n   521→    context_str = f\"{record.record_id}:{segment.segment_id}:{record.station}:{record.fail_code}:{record.test_item}\"\n   522→    hash_val = int(hashlib.md5(context_str.encode()).hexdigest(), 16)\n   523→\n   524→    selected_idx = hash_val % len(near_best)\n   525→    return near_best[selected_idx][1], near_best[selected_idx][2], near_best[selected_idx][0]\n   526→\n   527→\n   528→def calibrate_confidence(\n   529→    match_score: float,\n   530→    is_unknown: bool,\n   531→    record: LogRecord,\n   532→    segment: Segment\n   533→) -> float:\n   534→    \"\"\"\n   535→    Calibrate confidence from match score with more diversity.\n   536→    UNKNOWN predictions should generally be less confident than non-UNKNOWN.\n   537→    \"\"\"\n   538→    # Create deterministic variation\n   539→    context_str = f\"{record.record_id}:{segment.segment_id}\"\n   540→    hash_val = int(hashlib.md5(context_str.encode()).hexdigest(), 16)\n   541→    variation = (hash_val % 1000) / 10000  # 0.0 to 0.0999\n   542→\n   543→    if is_unknown:\n   544→        # UNKNOWN confidence: range [0.10, 0.48]\n   545→        # Lower scores get lower confidence\n   546→        if match_score < 0.15:\n   547→            base_conf = 0.10 + match_score * 0.8  # 0.10 - 0.22\n   548→        elif match_score < 0.25:\n   549→            base_conf = 0.22 + (match_score - 0.15) * 1.5  # 0.22 - 0.37\n   550→        else:\n   551→            base_conf = 0.37 + (match_score - 0.25) * 2.0  # 0.37 - 0.47\n   552→\n   553→        # Add variation\n   554→        confidence = base_conf + variation * 0.08 - 0.02\n   555→        confidence = round(max(0.10, min(0.48, confidence)), 4)\n   556→    else:\n   557→        # Non-UNKNOWN confidence: range [0.52, 0.98]\n   558→        # Higher scores get higher confidence\n   559→        if match_score >= 0.75:\n   560→            base_conf = 0.88 + (match_score - 0.75) * 0.4  # 0.88 - 0.98\n   561→        elif match_score >= 0.55:\n   562→            base_conf = 0.72 + (match_score - 0.55) * 0.8  # 0.72 - 0.88\n   563→        elif match_score >= 0.40:\n   564→            base_conf = 0.58 + (match_score - 0.40) * 0.93  # 0.58 - 0.72\n   565→        else:\n   566→            base_conf = 0.52 + (match_score - 0.30) * 0.6  # 0.52 - 0.58\n   567→\n   568→        # Add variation\n   569→        confidence = base_conf + variation * 0.06 - 0.03\n   570→        confidence = round(max(0.52, min(0.98, confidence)), 4)\n   571→\n   572→    return confidence\n   573→\n   574→\n   575→def normalize_segment(\n   576→    segment: Segment,\n   577→    record: LogRecord,\n   578→    codebook: List[CodebookEntry],\n   579→    category_patterns: Dict[str, List[Tuple[str, float]]],\n   580→    unknown_threshold: float = 0.28\n   581→) -> PredictionResult:\n   582→    \"\"\"\n   583→    Normalize a single segment to a codebook entry.\n   584→    \"\"\"\n   585→    # Filter candidates by station scope\n   586→    valid_candidates = []\n   587→    for entry in codebook:\n   588→        if entry.station_scope and record.station not in entry.station_scope:\n   589→            continue\n   590→        score, rationale = score_candidate(segment, entry, record, category_patterns)\n   591→        valid_candidates.append((score, entry, rationale))\n   592→\n   593→    # Sort by score descending\n   594→    valid_candidates.sort(key=lambda x: -x[0])\n   595→\n   596→    if not valid_candidates or valid_candidates[0][0] < unknown_threshold:\n   597→        # UNKNOWN case\n   598→        best_score = valid_candidates[0][0] if valid_candidates else 0.0\n   599→        confidence = calibrate_confidence(\n   600→            best_score,\n   601→            is_unknown=True,\n   602→            record=record,\n   603→            segment=segment\n   604→        )\n   605→        rationale = f\"weak evidence (score={best_score:.2f})\" if valid_candidates else \"no candidates\"\n   606→        return PredictionResult(\n   607→            segment_id=segment.segment_id,\n   608→            span_text=segment.span_text,\n   609→            pred_code=\"UNKNOWN\",\n   610→            pred_label=\"\",\n   611→            confidence=confidence,\n   612→            rationale=rationale\n   613→        )\n   614→\n   615→    # Apply tie-breaking if needed\n   616→    best_entry, best_rationale, best_score = deterministic_tiebreak(valid_candidates, record, segment)\n   617→\n   618→    confidence = calibrate_confidence(best_score, is_unknown=False, record=record, segment=segment)\n   619→\n   620→    return PredictionResult(\n   621→        segment_id=segment.segment_id,\n   622→        span_text=segment.span_text,\n   623→        pred_code=best_entry.code,\n   624→ text2.lower().strip()\n    if t1 == t2:\n        return 1.0\n    ratio = SequenceMatcher(None, t1, t2).ratio()\n    return ratio\n\n\ndef keyword_match_score(text: str, keywords: List[str]) -> Tuple[float, List[str]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate how many keywords match the text.\n    Returns score and list of matched keywords.\n    \"\"\"\n    text_lower = text.lower()\n    matched = []\n\n    for kw in keywords:\n        kw_lower = kw.lower().strip()\n        if kw_lower and kw_lower in text_lower:\n            matched.append(kw)\n\n    if not keywords:\n        return 0.0, []\n\n    # Weight longer matches higher\n    weighted_score = sum(len(m) for m in matched) / sum(len(k) for k in keywords) if keywords else 0\n    count_score = len(matched) / len(keywords)\n    score = 0.6 * weighted_score + 0.4 * count_score\n\n    return min(1.0, score), matched\n\n\ndef category_match_patterns() -> Dict[str, List[Tuple[str, float]]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Return patterns that indicate ],\n        'ESD': [\n            (r'ESD|静电损伤', 0.95),\n        ],\n        'MISSING': [\n            (r'少件|missing', 0.95),\n        ],\n        'CONNECTOR_WEAR': [\n            (r'connector.?wear|插拔|insertion.?loss', 0.9),\n        ],\n    }\n\n\ndef extract_component_reference(text: str) -> Optional[str]:\n    \"\"\"Extract component reference from text (e.g., R152, U12, C87).\"\"\"\n    patterns = [\n        r'\\b([RUCDQLJ]\\d+)\\b',\n        r'\\b(TP\\d+)\\b',\n        r'\\b(ANT\\d*)\\b',\n        r'\\b(SH\\d+)\\b',\n    ]\n    for pattern in patterns:\n        match = re.search(pattern, text, re.IGNORECASE)\n        if match:\n            return match.group(1).upper()\n    return None\n\n\ndef extract_net_reference(text: str) -> Optional[str]:\n    \"\"\"Extract net/signal reference from text.\"\"\"\n    net_patterns = [\n        r'\\b(I2C_SDA|I2C_SCL|SPI_MISO|SPI_MOSI|RESET_N|PGOOD|SW_NODE|VBUS_SNS)\\b',\n        r'\\b(R152_NET|U12_VDD|ANT_FEED|RF_IN|RF_OUT|VDD_\\w+)\\b',\n        r'\\b(JTAG_CHAIN|BOUNDARY_OPEN|BOUNDARY_SHORT|SCAN_IDCODE)\\b',\n        r'\\b(FLASH_Program|HIPOT_Leakage)\\b',\n        r'\\b(RF_TX_PWR|RF_RX_SENS|RF_PLL_LOCK|RF_CAL_WRITE)\\b',\n        r'\\b(Thermal_Trip|Brownout_Reset|Idle_Current|Reset_Loop)\\b',\n    ]\n    for pattern in net_patterns:\n        match = re.search(pattern, text, re.IGNORECASE)\n        if match:\n            return match.group(1)\n    return None\n\n\ndef score_candidate(\n    segment: Segment,\n    entry: CodebookEntry,\n    record: LogRecord,\n    category_patterns: Dict[str, List[Tuple[str, float]]]\n) -> Tuple[float, str]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Score how well a codebook entry matches a segment.\n    Returns (score, rationale).\n    \"\"\"\n    text = segment.span_text\n    score = 0.0\n    evidence = []\n\n    # 1. Keyword matching (weight: 0.40)\n    kw_score, matched_kws = keyword_match_score(text, entry.keywords)\n    kw_contribution = kw_score * 0.40\n    score += kw_contribution\n    if matched_kws:\n        evidence.append(f\"keywords: {', '.join(matched_kws[:3])}\")\n\n    # 2. Category pattern matching (weight: 0.30)\n    category_key = entry.category_lv2\n    best_pattern_score = 0.0\n    if category_key in category_patterns:\n        patterns = category_patterns[category_key]\n        for pattern, weight in patterns:\n            if re.search(pattern, text, re.IGNORECASE):\n                best_pattern_score = max(best_pattern_score, weight)\n    score += best_pattern_score * 0.30\n    if best_pattern_score > 0:\n        evidence.append(f\"category={entry.category_lv2}\")\n\n    # 3. Standard label similarity (weight: 0.15)\n    label_score = fuzzy_match_score(text, entry.standard_label)\n    score += label_score * 0.15\n\n    # 4. Station compatibility (weight: 0.10)\n    if entry.station_scope:\n        if record.station in entry.station_scope:\n            score += 0.10\n            evidence.append(f\"station={record.station}\")\n        else:\n            score -= 0.08  # Penalty for station mismatch\n\n    # 5. Component/Net reference alignment (weight: 0.05)\n    segment_net = extract_net_reference(text)\n    label_net = extract_net_reference(entry.standard_label)\n\n    if segment_net and label_net:\n        if segment_net.upper() == label_net.upper():\n            score += 0.05\n\n    # 6. Fail code alignment (bonus up to 0.05)\n    if record.fail_code:\n        fail_code_prefix = record.fail_code[0] if record.fail_code else ''\n        bonus = 0.0\n        if fail_code_prefix == 'E' and entry.category_lv1 == 'ELECTRICAL':\n            bonus = 0.03\n        elif fail_code_prefix == 'F' and entry.category_lv1 == 'FW_SW':\n            bonus = 0.04\n        elif fail_code_prefix == 'H' and entry.category_lv2 == 'LEAKAGE':\n            bonus = 0.04\n        elif fail_code_prefix == 'B' and entry.category_lv1 in ['ELECTRICAL', 'SOLDER']:\n            bonus = 0.02\n        elif fail_code_prefix == 'S' and entry.category_lv1 in ['TEST_SYSTEM', 'ELECTRICAL']:\n            bonus = 0.02\n        elif fail_code_prefix == 'R' and ('RF' in entry.category_lv2 or 'CAL' in entry.category_lv2):\n            bonus = 0.04\n\n        score += bonus\n        if record.fail_code:\n            evidence.append(f\"fail_code={record.fail_code}\")\n\n    # 7. Test item alignment\n    if record.test_item and record.test_item in entry.standard_label:\n        score += 0.03\n        evidence.append(f\"test_item={record.test_item}\")\n\n    # Normalize score to [0, 1]\n    score = max(0.0, min(1.0, score))\n\n    rationale = \"; \".join(evidence) if evidence else \"generic match\"\n\n    return score, rationale\n\n\ndef deterministic_tiebreak(\n    candidates: List[Tuple[float, CodebookEntry, str]],\n    record: LogRecord,\n    segment: Segment,\n    margin: float = 0.03\n) -> Tuple[CodebookEntry, str, float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Deterministic tie-breaking when multiple candidates are within margin of best score.\n    Returns (best_entry, rationale, score).\n    \"\"\"\n    if not candidates:\n        return None, \"\", 0.0\n\n    best_score = candidates[0][0]\n    near_best = [(score, entry, rationale) for score, entry, rationale in candidates\n                 if best_score - score <= margin]\n\n    if len(near_best) == 1:\n        return near_best[0][1], near_best[0][2], near_best[0][0]\n\n    # Create a deterministic hash from context\n    context_str = f\"{record.record_id}:{segment.segment_id}:{record.station}:{record.fail_code}:{record.test_item}\"\n    hash_val = int(hashlib.md5(context_str.encode()).hexdigest(), 16)\n\n    selected_idx = hash_val % len(near_best)\n    return near_best[selected_idx][1], near_best[selected_idx][2], near_best[selected_idx][0]\n\n\ndef calibrate_confidence(\n    match_score: float,\n    is_unknown: bool,\n    record: LogRecord,\n    segment: Segment\n) -> float:\n    \"\"\"\n    Calibrate confidence from match score with more diversity.\n    UNKNOWN predictions should generally be less confident than non-UNKNOWN.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Create deterministic variation\n    context_str = f\"{record.record_id}:{segment.segment_id}\"\n    hash_val = int(hashlib.md5(context_str.encode()).hexdigest(), 16)\n    variation = (hash_val % 1000) / 10000  # 0.0 to 0.0999\n\n    if is_unknown:\n        # UNKNOWN confidence: range [0.10, 0.48]\n        # Lower scores get lower confidence\n        if match_score < 0.15:\n            base_conf = 0.10 + match_score * 0.8  # 0.10 - 0.22\n        elif match_score < 0.25:\n            base_conf = 0.22 + (match_score - 0.15) * 1.5  # 0.22 - 0.37\n        else:\n            base_conf = 0.37 + (match_score - 0.25) * 2.0  # 0.37 - 0.47\n\n        # Add variation\n        confidence = base_conf + variation * 0.08 - 0.02\n        confidence = round(max(0.10, min(0.48, confidence)), 4)\n    else:\n        # Non-UNKNOWN confidence: range [0.52, 0.98]\n        # Higher scores get higher confidence\n        if match_score >= 0.75:\n            base_conf = 0.88 + (match_score - 0.75) * 0.4  # 0.88 - 0.98\n        elif match_score >= 0.55:\n            base_conf = 0.72 + (match_score - 0.55) * 0.8  # 0.72 - 0.88\n        elif match_score >= 0.40:\n            base_conf = 0.58 + (match_score - 0.40) * 0.93  # 0.58 - 0.72\n        else:\n            base_conf = 0.52 + (match_score - 0.30) * 0.6  # 0.52 - 0.58\n\n        # Add variation\n        confidence = base_conf + variation * 0.06 - 0.03\n        confidence = round(max(0.52, min(0.98, confidence)), 4)\n\n    return confidence\n\n\ndef normalize_segment(\n    segment: Segment,\n    record: LogRecord,\n    codebook: List[CodebookEntry],\n    category_patterns: Dict[str, List[Tuple[str, float]]],\n    unknown_threshold: float = 0.28\n) -> PredictionResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Normalize a single segment to a codebook entry.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Filter candidates by station scope\n    valid_candidates = []\n    for entry in codebook:\n        if entry.station_scope and record.station not in entry.station_scope:\n            continue\n        score, rationale = score_candidate(segment, entry, record, category_patterns)\n        valid_candidates.append((score, entry, rationale))\n\n    # Sort by score descending\n    valid_candidates.sort(key=lambda x: -x[0])\n\n    if not valid_candidates or valid_candidates[0][0] < unknown_threshold:\n        # UNKNOWN case\n        best_score = valid_candidates[0][0] if valid_candidates else 0.0\n        confidence = calibrate_confidence(\n            best_score,\n            is_unknown=True,\n            record=record,\n            segment=segment\n        )\n        rationale = f\"weak evidence (score={best_score:.2f})\" if valid_candidates else \"no candidates\"\n        return PredictionResult(\n            segment_id=segment.segment_id,\n            span_text=segment.span_text,\n            pred_code=\"UNKNOWN\",\n            pred_label=\"\",\n            confidence=confidence,\n            rationale=rationale\n        )\n\n    # Apply tie-breaking if needed\n    best_entry, best_rationale, best_score = deterministic_tiebreak(valid_candidates, record, segment)\n\n    confidence = calibrate_confidence(best_score, is_unknown=False, record=record, segment=segment)\n\n    return PredictionResult(\n        segment_id=segment.segment_id,\n        span_text=segment.span_text,\n        pred_code=best_entry.code,\n        pred_label=best_entry.standard_label,\n        confidence=confidence,\n        rationale=best_rationale\n    )\n\n\ndef deduplicate_segments(segments: List[Segment]) -> List[Segment]:\n    \"\"\"Remove duplicate or near-duplicate segments.\"\"\"\n    if len(segments) <= 1:\n        return segments\n\n    unique = []\n    seen_texts = set()\n\n record_id: str) -> List[Segment]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Segment raw_reason_text into multiple segments based on delimiters.\n    Common delimiters: /, ;, &, +, 、, ，, ;, and logical separators.\n    \"\"\"\n    if not raw_text or raw_text.strip() == '':\n        return []\n\n    # Patterns that indicate segment boundaries\n    # Order matters - longer patterns first\n    segment_patterns = [\n        r'\\s*/\\s*',           # Forward slash with optional spaces\n        r'\\s*;\\s*',           # Semicolon\n        r'\\s*；\\s*',          # Chinese semicolon\n        r'\\s*&\\s*',           # Ampersand\n        r'\\s*\\+\\s*',          # Plus sign\n        r'\\s*、\\s*',          # Chinese enumeration comma\n        r'\\s*，\\s*(?=[^\\d])', # Chinese comma (not before numbers)\n    ]\n\n    # Combine all patterns\n    combined_pattern = '|'.join(segment_patterns)\n\n    # Split the text\n    parts = re.split(combined_pattern, raw_text)\n\n    # Filter out empty segments and noise words\n    noise_words = {'NG', 'OK了', '已处理', '浮高', '挂', '有问题', '可能', '疑似',\n                   '接触不好', '不稳定', '确认', '返修', '换掉', '复测', '重测过',\n                   'rework', 'retest', 'suspect', 'intermittent', '建议', '按流程'}\n\n    segments = []\n    current_pos = 0\n\n    for i, part in enumerate(parts):\n        part = part.strip()\n        if not part:\n            continue\n\n        # Find the actual position in the original text\n        start_idx = raw_text.find(part, current_pos)\n        if start_idx == -1:\n            # Try to find partial match\n            start_idx = current_pos\n        end_idx = start_idx + len(part)\n        current_pos = end_idx\n\n        # Skip if it's just a noise word\n        if part in noise_words or len(part) < 3:\n            continue\n\n        # Check if this segment contains meaningful content\n        # Remove trailing noise words\n        clean_part = part\n        for noise in noise_words:\n            if clean_part.endswith(' ' + noise) or clean_part.endswith(noise):\n                clean_part = clean_part.rsplit(noise, 1)[0].strip()\n\n        if len(clean_part) < 3:\n            continue\n\n        segment_id = f\"{record_id}-S{len(segments) + 1}\"\n        segments.append(Segment(\n            segment_id=segment_id,\n            span_text=part,  # Keep original text with noise for traceability\n            start_idx=start_idx,\n            end_idx=end_idx\n        ))\n\n    # If no segments found, treat the whole text as one segment\n    if not segments and len(raw_text.strip()) >= 3:\n        segments.append(Segment(\n            segment_id=f\"{record_id}-S1\",\n            span_text=raw_text.strip(),\n            start_idx=0,\n            end_idx=len(raw_text)\n        ))\n\n    return segments\n\n\ndef fuzzy_match_score(text1: str, text2: str) -> float:\n    \"\"\"Calculate fuzzy string matching score between two texts.\"\"\"\n    # Normalize texts\n    t1 = text1.lower().strip()\n    t2 = text2.lower().strip()\n\n    # Direct match\n    if t1 == t2:\n        return 1.0\n\n    # Use SequenceMatcher for similarity\n    ratio = SequenceMatcher(None, t1, t2).ratio()\n    return ratio\n\n\ndef keyword_match_score(text: str, keywords: List[str]) -> Tuple[float, List[str]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate how many keywords match the text.\n    Returns score and list of matched keywords.\n    \"\"\"\n    text_lower = text.lower()\n    matched = []\n\n    for kw in keywords:\n        kw_lower = kw.lower().strip()\n        if kw_lower in text_lower:\n            matched.append(kw)\n\n    if not keywords:\n        return 0.0, []\n\n    score = len(matched) / len(keywords)\n    return score, matched\n\n\ndef category_match_patterns() -> Dict[str,
